# Skills · S · part 88

884 entries, self-contained-prose to self-improve. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **self-contained-prose**: Find and fix prose that only its authoring session can understand: citations of conversations, plans, tickets in source comments, private workspaces, or commits; "this PR" or "this increment" vantage; change narration such as "used to" or "no longer"; review arguments; and planning residue. Use when writing or reviewing code comments, documentation, reports, or slides that others will read without the session's context. ([Nyxze/dotfiles](https://github.com/Nyxze/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/self-contained-prose))
- **self-contained-python-script**: Write self-installing, self-contained Python scripts using uv and PEP 723. Covers PEP 723 inline metadata, ergonomic CLI design (argparse / Cyclopts), rich output formatting, progress bars (alive-progress), interactive prompts (questionary), and fuzzy selection (iterfzf). Use when asked to create a Python script, CLI tool, or automation script. ([boazy/skills](https://github.com/boazy/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/self-contained-python-scripts))
